+      <div class="status">MJPEG stream from the UNO Q camera</div>
+    </header>
+    <section class="viewer">
+      <img src="/stream.mjpg" alt="Live camera stream">
+    </section>
+    <nav class="actions">
+      <a href="/snapshot.jpg" target="_blank">Open snapshot</a>
+      <a href="/health" target="_blank">Health</a>
+    </nav>
+  </main>
+</body>
+</html>
+"""
+
+
+class CameraStream:
+    def __init__(self, camera_index: int):
+        self.camera_index = camera_index
+        self.capture = None
+        self.frame = None
+        self.error = "Camera not started"
+        self.lock = threading.Lock()
+        self.stop_event = threading.Event()
+        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._capture_loop, daemon=True)
+
+    def start(self) -> None:
+        self.thread.start()
+
+    def stop(self) -> None:
+        self.stop_event.set()
+        self.thread.join(timeout=2)
+        if self.capture is not None:
+            self.capture.release()
+
+    def get_jpeg(self):
+        with self.lock:
+            if self.frame is None:
+                return None
+            frame = self.frame.copy()
+
+        ok, encoded = cv2.imencode(
+            ".jpg",
+            frame,
+            [int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), JPEG_QUALITY],
+        )
+        if not ok:
+            return None
+        return encoded.tobytes()
+
+    def get_status(self) -> str:
+        with self.lock:
+            has_frame = self.frame is not None
+            error = self.error
+        if has_frame:
+            return "ok"
+        return error
+
+    def _capture_loop(self) -> None:
+        while not self.stop_event.is_set():
+            if self.capture is None or not self.capture.isOpened():
+                self._open_camera()
+                time.sleep(0.5)
+                continue
+
+            ok, frame = self.capture.read()
+            if not ok or frame is None:
+                with self.lock:
+                    self.error = "Failed to read frame from camera"
+                time.sleep(0.2)
+                continue
+
+            with self.lock:
+                self.frame = frame
+                self.error = ""
+
+            time.sleep(FRAME_INTERVAL_SEC)
+
+    def _open_camera(self) -> None:
+        if self.capture is not None:
+            self.capture.release()
+
+        capture = cv2.VideoCapture(self.camera_index)
+        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, FRAME_WIDTH)
+        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, FRAME_HEIGHT)
+
+        if not capture.isOpened():
+            with self.lock:
+                self.capture = None
+                self.error = f"Cannot open camera index {self.camera_index}"
+            return
+
+        with self.lock:
+            self.capture = capture
+            self.error = ""
+        print(f"Opened camera index {self.camera_index}")
+
+
+camera = CameraStream(CAMERA_INDEX)
+
+
+class CameraRequestHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+    def do_GET(self):
+        if self.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
+            self._send_html()
+        elif self.path == "/stream.mjpg":
+            self._send_stream()
+        elif self.path == "/snapshot.jpg":
+            self._send_snapshot()
+        elif self.path == "/health":
+            self._send_text(camera.get_status())
+        else:
+            self.send_error(HTTPStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Not found")
+
+    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
+        print(f"{self.address_string()} - {fmt % args}")
+
+    def _send_html(self):
+        body = INDEX_HTML.encode("utf-8")
+        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
+        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
+        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
+        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
+        self.end_headers()
+        self.wfile.write(body)
+
+    def _send_text(self, text: str):
+        body = f"{text}\n".encode("utf-8")
+        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
+        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8")
+        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
+        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
+        self.end_headers()
+        self.wfile.write(body)
+
+    def _send_snapshot(self):
+        jpeg = camera.get_jpeg()
+        if jpeg is None:
+            self.send_error(HTTPStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, camera.get_status())
+            return
+
+        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
+        self.send_header("Content-Type", "image/jpeg")
+        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(jpeg)))
+        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
+        self.end_headers()
+        self.wfile.write(jpeg)
+
+    def _send_stream(self):
+        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
+        self.send_header("Age", "0")
+        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-cache, private")
+        self.send_header("Pragma", "no-cache")
+        self.send_header("Content-Type", "multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=frame")
+        self.end_headers()
+
+        while True:
+            jpeg = camera.get_jpeg()
+            if jpeg is None:
+                time.sleep(0.2)
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                self.wfile.write(b"--frame\r\n")
+                self.wfile.write(b"Content-Type: image/jpeg\r\n")
+                self.wfile.write(f"Content-Length: {len(jpeg)}\r\n\r\n".encode("ascii"))
+                self.wfile.write(jpeg)
+                self.wfile.write(b"\r\n")
+            except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
+                break
+
+            time.sleep(FRAME_INTERVAL_SEC)
+
+
+def start_http_server() -> ThreadingHTTPServer:
+    server = ThreadingHTTPServer((HOST, PORT), CameraRequestHandler)
+    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
+    thread.start()
+    print(f"OpenCV camera server listening on http://0.0.0.0:{PORT}")
+    return server
+
+
+camera.start()
+server = start_http_server()
+
+
+def loop():
+    time.sleep(10)
+
+
+App.run(user_loop=loop)

import threading
import time
from http import HTTPStatus
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer

import cv2
from arduino.app_utils import App


HOST = "0.0.0.0"
PORT = 8080
CAMERA_INDEX = 0
FRAME_WIDTH = 640
FRAME_HEIGHT = 480
JPEG_QUALITY = 80
FRAME_INTERVAL_SEC = 1 / 30


INDEX_HTML = """<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>OpenCV Camera</title>

      gap: 16px;
    }

      display: flex;
      align-items: end;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 16px;
    }

    }

      border-radius: 6px;
      padding: 8px 12px;
      color: #102a43;
      background: #fff;
      text-decoration: none;
      font-size: 14px;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <main>
    <header>
      <h1>OpenCV Camera</h1>
      <div class="status">MJPEG stream from the UNO Q camera</div>
    </header>
    <section class="viewer">
      <img src="/stream.mjpg" alt="Live camera stream">
    </section>
    <nav class="actions">
      <a href="/snapshot.jpg" target="_blank">Open snapshot</a>
      <a href="/health" target="_blank">Health</a>
    </nav>
  </main>
</body>
</html>
"""


class CameraStream:
    def __init__(self, camera_index: int):
        self.camera_index = camera_index
        self.capture = None
        self.frame = None
        self.error = "Camera not started"
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.stop_event = threading.Event()
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._capture_loop, daemon=True)

    def start(self) -> None:
        self.thread.start()

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self.stop_event.set()
        self.thread.join(timeout=2)
        if self.capture is not None:
            self.capture.release()

    def get_jpeg(self):
        with self.lock:
            if self.frame is None:
                return None
            frame = self.frame.copy()

        ok, encoded = cv2.imencode(
            ".jpg",
            frame,
            [int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), JPEG_QUALITY],
        )
        if not ok:
            return None
        return encoded.tobytes()

    def get_status(self) -> str:
        with self.lock:
            has_frame = self.frame is not None
            error = self.error
        if has_frame:
            return "ok"
        return error

    def _capture_loop(self) -> None:
        while not self.stop_event.is_set():
            if self.capture is None or not self.capture.isOpened():
                self._open_camera()
                time.sleep(0.5)
                continue

            ok, frame = self.capture.read()
            if not ok or frame is None:
                with self.lock:
                    self.error = "Failed to read frame from camera"
                time.sleep(0.2)
                continue

            with self.lock:
                self.frame = frame
                self.error = ""

            time.sleep(FRAME_INTERVAL_SEC)

    def _open_camera(self) -> None:
        if self.capture is not None:
            self.capture.release()

        capture = cv2.VideoCapture(self.camera_index)
        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, FRAME_WIDTH)
        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, FRAME_HEIGHT)

        if not capture.isOpened():
            with self.lock:
                self.capture = None
                self.error = f"Cannot open camera index {self.camera_index}"
            return

        with self.lock:
            self.capture = capture
            self.error = ""
        print(f"Opened camera index {self.camera_index}")


camera = CameraStream(CAMERA_INDEX)


class CameraRequestHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            self._send_html()
        elif self.path == "/stream.mjpg":
            self._send_stream()
        elif self.path == "/snapshot.jpg":
            self._send_snapshot()
        elif self.path == "/health":
            self._send_text(camera.get_status())
        else:
            self.send_error(HTTPStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Not found")

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        print(f"{self.address_string()} - {fmt % args}")

    def _send_html(self):
        body = INDEX_HTML.encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _send_text(self, text: str):
        body = f"{text}\n".encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _send_snapshot(self):
        jpeg = camera.get_jpeg()
        if jpeg is None:
            self.send_error(HTTPStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, camera.get_status())
            return

        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "image/jpeg")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(jpeg)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(jpeg)

    def _send_stream(self):
        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
        self.send_header("Age", "0")
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-cache, private")
        self.send_header("Pragma", "no-cache")
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=frame")
        self.end_headers()

        while True:
            jpeg = camera.get_jpeg()
            if jpeg is None:
                time.sleep(0.2)
                continue

            try:
                self.wfile.write(b"--frame\r\n")
                self.wfile.write(b"Content-Type: image/jpeg\r\n")
                self.wfile.write(f"Content-Length: {len(jpeg)}\r\n\r\n".encode("ascii"))
                self.wfile.write(jpeg)
                self.wfile.write(b"\r\n")
            except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):

FRAME_HEIGHT = 480
JPEG_QUALITY = 80
FRAME_INTERVAL_SEC = 1 / 30


INDEX_HTML = """<!doctype html>

      <a href="/snapshot.jpg" target="_blank">Open snapshot</a>
      <a href="/health" target="_blank">Health</a>
    </nav>
  </main>
</body>
</html>
"""


class CameraStream:
    def __init__(self, camera_index: int):
        self.camera_index = camera_index
        self.capture = None
        self.frame = None
        self.error = "Camera not started"
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.stop_event = threading.Event()
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._capture_loop, daemon=True)

    def start(self) -> None:
        self.thread.start()

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self.stop_event.set()
        self.thread.join(timeout=2)
        if self.capture is not None:
            self.capture.release()

    def get_jpeg(self):
        with self.lock:
            if self.frame is None:
                return None
            frame = self.frame.copy()

        ok, encoded = cv2.imencode(
            ".jpg",
            frame,
            [int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), JPEG_QUALITY],
        )
        if not ok:
            return None
        return encoded.tobytes()

    def get_status(self) -> str:
        with self.lock:
            has_frame = self.frame is not None
            error = self.error
        if has_frame:
            return "ok"
        return error

    def _capture_loop(self) -> None:
        while not self.stop_event.is_set():
            if self.capture is None or not self.capture.isOpened():
                self._open_camera()
                time.sleep(0.5)
                continue

            ok, frame = self.capture.read()
            if not ok or frame is None:
                with self.lock:
                    self.error = "Failed to read frame from camera"
                time.sleep(0.2)
                continue

            with self.lock:
                self.frame = frame
                self.error = ""

            time.sleep(FRAME_INTERVAL_SEC)

    def _open_camera(self) -> None:
        if self.capture is not None:
            self.capture.release()

        capture = cv2.VideoCapture(self.camera_index)
        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, FRAME_WIDTH)
        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, FRAME_HEIGHT)

        if not capture.isOpened():
            with self.lock:
                self.capture = None
                self.error = f"Cannot open camera index {self.camera_index}"
            return

        with self.lock:
            self.capture = capture
            self.error = ""
        print(f"Opened camera index {self.camera_index}")


camera = CameraStream(CAMERA_INDEX)


class CameraRequestHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            self._send_html()
        elif self.path == "/stream.mjpg":

            self._send_text(camera.get_status())
        else:
            self.send_error(HTTPStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Not found")

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        print(f"{self.address_string()} - {fmt % args}")

    def _send_html(self):
        body = INDEX_HTML.encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _send_text(self, text: str):
        body = f"{text}\n".encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()

    def _send_snapshot(self):

        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)

    def _send_stream(self):
        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.OK)
        self.send_header("Age", "0")
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-cache, private")
        self.send_header("Pragma", "no-cache")
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=frame")

            except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                break

+class OpenCVCamera:
+    def __init__(self, camera_index: int):
+        self.camera_index = camera_index
+        self.capture = None
+        self.status = "Camera not started"
+
+    def open(self) -> bool:
+        if self.capture is not None and self.capture.isOpened():
+            return True
+
+        if self.capture is not None:
+            self.capture.release()
+
+        capture = cv2.VideoCapture(self.camera_index)
+        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, FRAME_WIDTH)
+        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, FRAME_HEIGHT)
+
+        if not capture.isOpened():
+            self.capture = None
+            self.status = f"Cannot open camera index {self.camera_index}"
+            return False
+

+        self.status = "Camera opened"
+        print(self.status)
+        return True

+            [int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), JPEG_QUALITY],

+            self.status = "Failed to encode frame as JPEG"
+            return None
+
+        self.status = "Streaming"
+        return encoded.tobytes()

+    def close(self) -> None:

+
+def send_status() -> None:
+    ui.send_message("status", message={
+        "text": camera.status,
+        "camera_index": CAMERA_INDEX,
+        "width": FRAME_WIDTH,
+        "height": FRAME_HEIGHT,
+    })
+
+
+def send_frame() -> None:
+    jpeg = camera.read_jpeg()
+    if jpeg is None:
+        send_status()
+        time.sleep(0.5)
+        return
+
+    frame_base64 = base64.b64encode(jpeg).decode("ascii")
+    ui.send_message("frame", message={
+        "image": f"data:image/jpeg;base64,{frame_base64}",
+        "status": camera.status,
+    })
+    time.sleep(FRAME_INTERVAL_SEC)
+
+
+def handle_client_connected(sid):
+    print(f"Web UI connected: {sid}")
+    send_status()
+
+
+ui.on_connect(handle_client_connected)
+
+
+def loop():
+    send_frame()
+
+
+print("OpenCV WebUI camera app started.")
+App.run(user_loop=loop)

*** Add File: Apps/opencv-detection/assets/app.js
+const socket = io();
+
+const cameraFrame = document.getElementById('cameraFrame');
+const emptyState = document.getElementById('emptyState');
+const statusText = document.getElementById('statusText');
+const connectionBadge = document.getElementById('connectionBadge');
+const frameCounter = document.getElementById('frameCounter');
+const cameraInfo = document.getElementById('cameraInfo');
+
+let framesReceived = 0;
+
+function setConnectionState(label, isConnected) {
+  connectionBadge.textContent = label;
+  connectionBadge.dataset.connected = isConnected ? 'true' : 'false';
+}
+
+socket.on('connect', () => {
+  setConnectionState('Connected', true);
+});
+
+socket.on('disconnect', () => {
+  setConnectionState('Disconnected', false);
+  statusText.textContent = 'Connection to the board lost.';
+});
+
+socket.on('status', (message) => {
+  statusText.textContent = message.text || 'Waiting for camera frames...';
+  cameraInfo.textContent = `Camera ${message.camera_index} - ${message.width} x ${message.height}`;
+});
+
+socket.on('frame', (message) => {
+  if (!message.image) {
+    return;
+  }
+
+  framesReceived += 1;
+  cameraFrame.src = message.image;
+  cameraFrame.style.display = 'block';
+  emptyState.style.display = 'none';
+  statusText.textContent = message.status || 'Streaming';
+  frameCounter.textContent = `${framesReceived} frames`;
+});

import base64
import time

import cv2
from arduino.app_bricks.web_ui import WebUI
from arduino.app_utils import App


CAMERA_INDEX = 0
FRAME_WIDTH = 640
FRAME_HEIGHT = 480
JPEG_QUALITY = 75
FRAME_INTERVAL_SEC = 1 / 15


ui = WebUI()


class OpenCVCamera:
    def __init__(self, camera_index: int):
        self.camera_index = camera_index
        self.capture = None
        self.status = "Camera not started"

    def open(self) -> bool:
        if self.capture is not None and self.capture.isOpened():
            return True

        if self.capture is not None:
            self.capture.release()

        capture = cv2.VideoCapture(self.camera_index)
        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, FRAME_WIDTH)
        capture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, FRAME_HEIGHT)

        if not capture.isOpened():
            self.capture = None
            self.status = f"Cannot open camera index {self.camera_index}"
            return False

        self.capture = capture
        self.status = "Camera opened"
        print(self.status)
        return True

    def read_jpeg(self):
        if not self.open():
            return None

        ok, frame = self.capture.read()
        if not ok or frame is None:
            self.status = "Failed to read frame from camera"
            return None

        encode_ok, encoded = cv2.imencode(
            ".jpg",
            frame,
            [int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), JPEG_QUALITY],
        )
        if not encode_ok:
            self.status = "Failed to encode frame as JPEG"
            return None

        self.status = "Streaming"
        return encoded.tobytes()

    def close(self) -> None:
        if self.capture is not None:
            self.capture.release()
            self.capture = None
        self.status = "Camera closed"


camera = OpenCVCamera(CAMERA_INDEX)


def send_status() -> None:
    ui.send_message("status", message={
        "text": camera.status,
        "camera_index": CAMERA_INDEX,
        "width": FRAME_WIDTH,
        "height": FRAME_HEIGHT,
    })


def send_frame() -> None:
    jpeg = camera.read_jpeg()
    if jpeg is None:
        send_status()
        time.sleep(0.5)
        return

    frame_base64 = base64.b64encode(jpeg).decode("ascii")
    ui.send_message("frame", message={
        "image": f"data:image/jpeg;base64,{frame_base64}",
        "status": camera.status,
    })
    time.sleep(FRAME_INTERVAL_SEC)


def handle_client_connected(sid):
    print(f"Web UI connected: {sid}")
    send_status()


ui.on_connect(handle_client_connected)


def loop():
    send_frame()


print("OpenCV WebUI camera app started.")
App.run(user_loop=loop)

Assistant 2026-06-05T15:55:03.217Z

已按你新增的 arduino:web_ui Brick 重构完成。

现在 opencv-detection 不再自建 HTTP/MJPEG 服务，而是使用 WebUI Brick：

  • python/main.py
  • 初始化 WebUI()。
  • 使用 OpenCV cv2.VideoCapture(0) 读取摄像头。
  • 将每帧编码为 JPEG。
  • Base64 后通过 ui.send_message("frame", ...) 推送到前端。

  • 摄像头状态通过 ui.send_message("status", ...) 推送。

  • 新增前端资源：

  • assets/index.html
  • assets/app.js

  • assets/style.css

  • app.yaml

  • 保留了你添加的：
bricks:
- arduino:web_ui: {}
  • ports 仍为空，由 WebUI Brick 管理页面服务。

运行方式：

  1. 在 App Lab 启动 opencv-detection。
  2. 打开 App Lab 提供的 WebUI 页面。
  3. 页面会通过 Socket.IO 接收 OpenCV 摄像头帧并显示。

Commentary 2026-06-05T15:59:21.169Z

日志里关键问题是这一行：unsupported version of the Socket.IO or Engine.IO protocols。这通常是前端加载的 Socket.IO 客户端版本和 WebUI Brick 后端不匹配导致的，所以页面连不上后端，自然收不到 frame 事件。我会改成使用项目内官方示例同款的 socket.io.min.js，并把帧率/分辨率降到更稳的预览参数。

const socket = io();

const cameraFrame = document.getElementById('cameraFrame');
const emptyState = document.getElementById('emptyState');
const statusText = document.getElementById('statusText');
const connectionBadge = document.getElementById('connectionBadge');
const frameCounter = document.getElementById('frameCounter');
const cameraInfo = document.getElementById('cameraInfo');

let framesReceived = 0;

function setConnectionState(label, isConnected) {
  connectionBadge.textContent = label;
  connectionBadge.dataset.connected = isConnected ? 'true' : 'false';
}

socket.on('connect', () => {
  setConnectionState('Connected', true);
});

socket.on('disconnect', () => {
  setConnectionState('Disconnected', false);
  statusText.textContent = 'Connection to the board lost.';
});

socket.on('status', (message) => {
  statusText.textContent = message.text || 'Waiting for camera frames...';
  cameraInfo.textContent = `Camera ${message.camera_index} - ${message.width} x ${message.height}`;
});

socket.on('frame', (message) => {
  if (!message.image) {
    return;
  }

  framesReceived += 1;
  cameraFrame.src = message.image;
  cameraFrame.style.display = 'block';
  emptyState.style.display = 'none';
  statusText.textContent = message.status || 'Streaming';
  frameCounter.textContent = `${framesReceived} frames`;
});

const socket = io();

const cameraFrame = document.getElementById('cameraFrame');
const emptyState = document.getElementById('emptyState');
const statusText = document.getElementById('statusText');
const connectionBadge = document.getElementById('connectionBadge');
const frameCounter = document.getElementById('frameCounter');
const cameraInfo = document.getElementById('cameraInfo');

let framesReceived = 0;

function setConnectionState(label, isConnected) {
  connectionBadge.textContent = label;
  connectionBadge.dataset.connected = isConnected ? 'true' : 'false';
}

socket.on('connect', () => {
  setConnectionState('Connected', true);
  statusText.textContent = 'Connected. Waiting for camera frames...';
});

socket.on('disconnect', () => {
  setConnectionState('Disconnected', false);
  statusText.textContent = 'Connection to the board lost.';
});

socket.on('connect_error', (error) => {
  setConnectionState('Connection error', false);
  statusText.textContent = error && error.message ? error.message : 'Socket.IO connection error.';
});

socket.on('status', (message) => {
  statusText.textContent = message.text || 'Waiting for camera frames...';
  cameraInfo.textContent = `Camera ${message.camera_index} - ${message.width} x ${message.height}`;
});

socket.on('frame', (message) => {
  if (!message.image) {
    return;
  }

  framesReceived += 1;
  cameraFrame.src = message.image;
  cameraFrame.style.display = 'block';
  emptyState.style.display = 'none';
  statusText.textContent = message.status || 'Streaming';
  frameCounter.textContent = `${framesReceived} frames`;
});

const socket = io(`http://${window.location.host}`);

const cameraFrame = document.getElementById('cameraFrame');
const emptyState = document.getElementById('emptyState');
const statusText = document.getElementById('statusText');
const connectionBadge = document.getElementById('connectionBadge');
const frameCounter = document.getElementById('frameCounter');
const cameraInfo = document.getElementById('cameraInfo');
const errorContainer = document.getElementById('error-container');

let framesReceived = 0;

function setConnectionState(label, isConnected) {
  connectionBadge.textContent = label;
  connectionBadge.dataset.connected = isConnected ? 'true' : 'false';
}

socket.on('connect', () => {
  setConnectionState('Connected', true);
  statusText.textContent = 'Connected. Waiting for camera frames...';
  errorContainer.style.display = 'none';
  errorContainer.textContent = '';
});

socket.on('disconnect', () => {
  setConnectionState('Disconnected', false);
  statusText.textContent = 'Connection to the board lost.';
  errorContainer.textContent = 'Connection to the board lost. Please check the App status.';
  errorContainer.style.display = 'block';
});

socket.on('connect_error', (error) => {
  setConnectionState('Connection error', false);
  statusText.textContent = error && error.message ? error.message : 'Socket.IO connection error.';
  errorContainer.textContent = statusText.textContent;
  errorContainer.style.display = 'block';
});

socket.on('status', (message) => {
  statusText.textContent = message.text || 'Waiting for camera frames...';
  cameraInfo.textContent = `Camera ${message.camera_index} - ${message.width} x ${message.height}`;
});

socket.on('frame', (message) => {
  if (!message.image) {
    return;
  }

  framesReceived += 1;
  cameraFrame.src = message.image;
  cameraFrame.style.display = 'block';
  emptyState.style.display = 'none';
  statusText.textContent = message.status || 'Streaming';
  frameCounter.textContent = `${framesReceived} frames`;
});

Apps/opencv-detection/assets/libs/socket.io.min.js

这样应该能解决你日志里的问题：

The client is using an unsupported version of the Socket.IO or Engine.IO protocols

我还做了两个稳定性调整：

  • OpenCV 推送参数降到 320 x 240、约 8 FPS、JPEG 质量 60，避免 WebUI Socket.IO 传 Base64 图片太重。
  • 页面加了错误提示区，如果 Socket 连接失败，会直接显示在页面顶部。

现在请重新启动 opencv-detection，然后打开 WebUI 页面：

http://10.233.80.145:7000

建议用无痕窗口，或强制刷新页面，避免浏览器缓存旧的 JS。
如果页面顶部显示 Connected. Waiting for camera frames... 但仍然没有画面，再看 App 日志里是否持续出现摄像头读取失败；如果显示 Streaming 但黑屏，那就是前端收到帧但图片渲染还有问题。
